printf '\c%#1.7%A|%+#8.0y\n' XXX 2>&1 | od -c | head -4; echo "[$?]"
printf 'x%0.3b\c%+#.0Gx' 9999999999999999999 -1 + 2>&1 | od -c | head -4; echo "[$?]"
printf 'x%##0.0s%3.7G\0101%-08.x~' 0b11 2>&1 | od -c | head -4; echo "[$?]"
printf 'A|%3.3m%%%0.*c\x41%##1.7nA|' 2>&1 | od -c | head -4; echo "[$?]"
